
======================================================================
FILE: cli/CONTRACT_ARCHITECTURE.md
======================================================================

# Toob Ecosystem Contracts & Ports Architecture

This document serves as the architectural single source of truth for how system boundaries (Contracts/Ports) are defined, verified, and enforced across the Toob ecosystem.

## 1. The `ports.go` Philosophy

In both the CLI and the Orchestrator, we use a dedicated `ports.go` file. This file acts as an **Interface Contract Registry**. It is the absolute source of truth for any data structure that crosses a system boundary (e.g., JSON Webhooks, Docker Labels, HTTP API Requests/Responses).

### The Golden Rules of Tagging
Every exported field inside a port struct **must** contain a `port` struct tag.
- `port:"required"`: The field is strictly required by the contract. Adding or changing a required field, or removing *any* field, is considered a **BREAKING CHANGE**.
- `port:"optional"`: The field is optional. Adding a new optional field is considered a **NON-BREAKING CHANGE**.

---

## 2. Toob-CLI: The SemVer Enforcer Workflow

The `toob-cli` is a distributed binary. Because users might run an older version of the CLI against a newer Compiler Container, we must prevent and detect **Version Skew**.

### Triggers & CI Integration
- **When:** The SemVer Enforcer runs automatically as a GitHub Action (`semver-enforcer.yml`) whenever a Pull Request modifies files in `cli/toob-cli/**`.
- **How it works:** 
  1. The pipeline fetches the `ports.go` from the `HEAD~1` baseline (the state before the PR).
  2. It runs the AST-based diffing tool (`cmd/semver`).
  3. The tool compares the old Abstract Syntax Tree against the new one.

### Enforcement Logic
- **Breaking Changes:** If the AST tool detects a breaking change (e.g., adding a `port:"required"` field, removing a field, changing a type), it forces the pipeline to check if the `ProtocolVersion` constant was manually incremented. **If not, the CI pipeline fails.**
- **Non-Breaking Changes:** Adding a `port:"optional"` field yields a `PATCH` or `MINOR` resolution, which passes without requiring a `ProtocolVersion` bump.

### Local Guards (`assertions_test.go`)
While the AST tool guards the external contract, `assertions_test.go` (run via `go test ./internal/ports/...`) guards the **internal consistency**. It uses Go reflection to ensure that the external `ports.go` structs and the internal implementation structs (e.g., `types.go`) have matching field counts and types, preventing them from silently drifting apart during local development.

---

## 3. Toob-CI Orchestrator: Co-Deployed Parity

The Orchestrator (`toob-ci`) has a fundamentally different deployment model. The Orchestrator daemon and the Compiler Containers it spawns are **co-deployed** directly from the same repository via `docker-compose`. 

Because they are built and started together, there is **no Version Skew possible**. (e.g., You will never have a v1 Orchestrator accidentally talking to a v2 Compiler Container).

### Triggers & CI Integration
- **When:** There is **no CI-driven SemVer Oracle pipeline** for the Orchestrator. 
- **Why:** Since there is no version skew and no external distribution, bumping a `ProtocolVersion` is meaningless dead code here.

### Local Guards (`assertions_test.go`)
Instead of a CI pipeline, the Orchestrator relies entirely on local developer guards run via `go test ./...`:

1. **`TestPortFieldParity`**: 
   Port structs (e.g., `WebhookReleasePayload`) and implementation structs (e.g., `ReleasePayload`) often represent different abstraction layers and thus have different field counts. This test ensures that any fields they **share** in common maintain the exact same Go data types.
2. **`TestSelfContainedPortsHaveTags`**: 
   A strict linter that iterates over all Orchestrator port structs (all 28 of them, covering GitHub APIs, Docker configs, and Daemon environment variables) and enforces that every single exported field has a valid `port:"required"` or `port:"optional"` tag.

### Summary of Orchestrator Boundaries
The `ports.go` in the pipeline repository explicitly documents the boundaries for:
- Orchestrator ↔ Compiler Container (via Docker Sockets/Volumes)
- Orchestrator ↔ GitHub API (Status Updates)
- GitHub ↔ Orchestrator (Incoming Webhooks)
- Orchestrator ↔ Registry (Git/File I/O)
- Hub API ↔ Clients (HTTP JSON endpoints)
- Daemon Environment Configuration


======================================================================
FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/docker-compose.yml
======================================================================

services:
  toob-ci:
    build: .
    container_name: toob-ci
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:9000:9000"
    environment:
      - WEBHOOK_SECRET=${WEBHOOK_SECRET:-SuperSecretDefaultTokenPleaseChangeMe}
      - GITHUB_TOKEN=${GITHUB_TOKEN:-}
      - TARGET_RELEASE_REPO=Toob-Boot/Toob-CLI-Release
      # Absolute host path for sibling container volume mounts (DooD)
      - HOST_REPO_PATH=${HOST_REPO_PATH:-/root/Toob-Loader}
    volumes:
      # Docker Socket for spawning sibling containers (release-runner + compiler sessions)
      - /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock
      # Mount the Toob-Loader repository root
      - ../../:/repo
      # Separate clone for registry (avoids git working tree conflicts)
      - toob_registry_clone:/repo-registry
      # Shared cache volumes (also mounted into sibling containers)
      - toob_go_cache:/cache/go
      - toob_cli_cache:/cache/cli
      - toob_logs:/cache/logs
      # Dockerfile.release + Dockerfile.compiler accessible for auto-build in start.sh
      - ./Dockerfile.release:/app/Dockerfile.release:ro
      - ./Dockerfile.compiler:/app/Dockerfile.compiler:ro
      - ./toob-ci-build.sh:/app/toob-ci-build.sh:ro
    logging:
      driver: "json-file"
      options:
        max-size: "10m"
        max-file: "3"
    # Orchestrator is now lightweight (no act). 300M is generous for pure Go + docker-cli.
    deploy:
      resources:
        limits:
          memory: 300M
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "wget", "-q", "--spider", "http://localhost:9000/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 3
    security_opt:
      - no-new-privileges:true

  caddy:
    image: caddy:2-alpine
    container_name: caddy-proxy
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "80:80"
      - "443:443"
    command: caddy reverse-proxy --from ci.the-toob.com --to toob-ci:9000
    depends_on:
      - toob-ci

volumes:
  toob_go_cache:
    name: toob_go_cache
  toob_cli_cache:
    name: toob_cli_cache
  toob_registry_clone:
    name: toob_registry_clone
  toob_logs:
    name: toob_logs
  # Shared between both compiler slots. Auto-downloaded toolchains are cached here.
  # Atomic rename in toolchain.EnsureAvailable() prevents corruption on parallel downloads.
  toob_toolchain_cache:
    name: toob_toolchain_cache

networks:
  toob-pr-net:
    driver: bridge
    internal: true

FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/Dockerfile

FROM golang:1.26-alpine AS builder

WORKDIR /build
COPY toob-ci/ ./toob-ci/
RUN cd toob-ci && go build -o /build/toob-ci-daemon .

FROM alpine:3.23

RUN apk add --no-cache docker-cli bash git wget go

COPY --from=builder /build/toob-ci-daemon /usr/local/bin/toob-ci
COPY start.sh /app/start.sh
RUN chmod +x /app/start.sh

EXPOSE 9000

CMD ["/app/start.sh"]

# Local Publisher Pipeline Setup (GitOps / Docker)

Diese Dokumentation beschreibt, wie du den "Toob Publisher Daemon" auf deinem Backend-Server ausrollst. 
Das System nutzt `nektos/act` verpackt in einen Docker-Container. Das bedeutet: Zero Install auf deinem Host-System, automatische Webhook-Verarbeitung und absolute Supply Chain Security durch Wegwerf-VMs.

> **Sicherheitshinweis (Zero-Keys-in-Repo):**  
> In diesem gesamten Setup landet **kein einziger Key oder Secret** in deinem Git-Repository. Alle Passwörter und GitHub-Tokens werden lokal auf dem Server in Umgebungsvariablen (`export`) oder in der Datei `.secrets` gespeichert. Die `.secrets`-Datei ist fest in der `.gitignore` verankert und verlässt deinen Server niemals.

## 1. Secrets hinterlegen

Damit `act` die fertigen Binaries in das externe Release-Repo pushen darf, speichern wir deinen Token in einer lokalen `.secrets` Datei auf dem Server.

1. Erstelle auf GitHub einen "Fine-grained Personal Access Token" (PAT).
2. Gib dem Token **nur** Zugriff auf das Repository `Toob-CLI-Release` und setze die Berechtigung `Contents: Write`.
3. Erstelle auf deinem Server im Root-Verzeichnis deines Repos (`Toob-Loader/.secrets`) die Datei:
   ```text
   RELEASE_TARGET_TOKEN=dein_github_token_hier
   ```

*(Hinweis: `.secrets` wird durch die `.gitignore` automatisch von Git ignoriert!)*

## 2. Den Daemon starten (Private Repo Deployment)

Damit deine Infrastruktur unsichtbar bleibt, haben wir den Daemon in ein privates GitHub-Repo ausgelagert (`Toob-CLI-Pipeline`).

Logge dich auf deinem Server ein, navigiere in das `Toob-Loader` Repository und klone das private Pipeline-Repo als versteckten Ordner (die `.gitignore` verhindert, dass es öffentlich gepusht wird):

```bash
cd cli
git clone https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-CLI-Pipeline .pipeline-repo
cd .pipeline-repo
export WEBHOOK_SECRET="DeinGeheimesPasswortFürDenWebhook"
docker-compose up -d --build
```

Damit läuft der Daemon ressourcenschonend im Hintergrund und lauscht auf Port `9000`.

## 3. GitHub Webhook einrichten

Damit der Daemon weiß, wann er loslegen soll:
1. Gehe auf GitHub in die Settings deines `Toob-Loader` Repositories.
2. Gehe auf **Webhooks** -> **Add webhook**.
3. **Payload URL:** `http://dein-server.com:9000/hooks/release`
4. **Content type:** `application/json`
5. **Secret:** Das Passwort, das du im `WEBHOOK_SECRET` vergeben hast.
6. **Trigger:** Wähle "Let me select individual events" und hake **nur** `Branch or tag creation` an.

## 4. Wie funktioniert der Release-Prozess jetzt?

1. Du pusht lokal auf deinem Laptop einen neuen Tag im offenen Repo:
   ```bash
   git tag v1.1.0
   git push origin v1.1.0
   ```
2. GitHub schickt sofort einen Ping an deinen Server (Port 9000).
3. Der `toob-publisher` Docker-Container prüft das kryptografische Secret.
4. Er startet über den Docker-Socket eine temporäre, isolierte Ubuntu-VM ("Sibling Container").
5. Der Code wird hermetisch für Windows, Linux und macOS kompiliert, signiert und hochgeladen.
6. Die Ubuntu-VM zerstört sich spurlos selbst.

## Updates des Daemons (Auto-Updater via Cronjob)

Wenn du an der unsichtbaren Infrastruktur (dem `Toob-CLI-Pipeline` Repo) etwas änderst, sorgt der eingebettete Poller (`auto-updater.sh`) dafür, dass sich der Server selbst aktualisiert.

**So richtest du die Automatik auf dem Server ein:**
1. Mach das Skript ausführbar:
   ```bash
   chmod +x cli/.pipeline-repo/auto-updater.sh
   ```
2. Öffne deine Cronjob-Verwaltung auf dem Server:
   ```bash
   crontab -e
   ```
3. Füge folgende Zeile ein (Pfade anpassen!), damit das Skript jede Minute den privaten Repo-Status prüft:
   ```bash
   * * * * * /absoluter/pfad/zu/Toob-Loader/cli/.pipeline-repo/auto-updater.sh >> /tmp/toob-updater.log 2>&1
   ```

Damit hast du ein 100% privates, wartungsfreies System. Wenn du am privaten Spawner-Repo etwas anpasst und pushst, updatet sich dein Server innerhalb von 60 Sekunden vollautomatisch!

# Toob-CLI-Pipeline

======================================================================
FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/start.sh
======================================================================

#!/bin/bash
set -e

echo "[Toob-CI] Cleaning up zombie containers from previous runs..."
docker ps -aq --filter "name=^slot-.*" | xargs -r docker rm -f 2>/dev/null || true
docker rm -f release-runner 2>/dev/null || true

# Ensure the PR-jail network exists
docker network create toob-pr-net 2>/dev/null || true

# Build the release-runner image if it doesn't exist locally
if ! docker image inspect toob-release-runner:latest >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "[Toob-CI] Building release-runner image..."
    docker build -t toob-release-runner:latest -f /app/Dockerfile.release /app/
fi

# Compiler images are pulled from DockerHub (mannomannx/toob-compiler) at runtime.

# Initialize registry clone if it doesn't exist yet.
# This gives the Matrix-Farm its own isolated git working tree
# so Release-Pipeline's `git reset --hard` never touches it.
if [ -f "/repo/.secrets" ]; then
    TOKEN=$(grep RELEASE_TARGET_TOKEN /repo/.secrets | cut -d '=' -f2 | tr -d '\r' | xargs)
fi

if [ -n "$TOKEN" ]; then
    GIT_URL="https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry.git"
    git config --global http.extraheader "Authorization: Basic $(echo -n x-access-token:${TOKEN} | base64)"
else
    GIT_URL="https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry.git"
fi

if [ ! -d "/repo-registry/.git" ]; then
    echo "[Toob-CI] First boot: Cloning toob-registry into /repo-registry..."
    git clone $GIT_URL /repo-registry
else
    echo "[Toob-CI] Registry clone found. Pulling latest..."
    cd /repo-registry
    git remote set-url origin $GIT_URL
	git pull --rebase || true
fi

# Compile registry scripts to avoid 'go run' overhead in the daemon
echo "[Toob-CI] Compiling internal registry scripts to native binaries..."
cd /repo-registry
go build -mod=mod -o /usr/local/bin/toob-matrix-generator scripts/matrix_generator.go
go build -mod=mod -o /usr/local/bin/toob-update-ledger scripts/update_ledger.go
go build -mod=mod -o /usr/local/bin/toob-clear-unverified scripts/clear_unverified.go

# Configure identity for commits
git config --global user.name "Toob-CI"
git config --global user.email "ci@toob-boot.io"

exec toob-ci


======================================================================
FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci-build.sh
======================================================================

#!/bin/bash
set -e

CHIP="$1"
if [ -z "$CHIP" ]; then
    echo "[toob-ci-build] FATAL: No chip specified." >&2
    exit 1
fi

CORE="${TOOB_CORE_VERSION:-main}"
COMPILER="${TOOB_COMPILER_VERSION:-latest}"
MODE="${TOOB_BUILD_MODE:-ci-test}"

echo "[toob-ci-build] Mode=$MODE Chip=$CHIP Core=$CORE Compiler=$COMPILER"

# The container mounts a tmpfs at /root/.toob.
# We populate the registry from our baked-in Docker image cache to make operations lightning fast.
# The CLI expects it in versions/main/ since we are testing against the main registry.
mkdir -p /root/.toob/registry/versions/main
cp -a /root/registry_cache/* /root/.toob/registry/versions/main/
# Create the symlink for latest/current version to satisfy CLI lookups
ln -sf /root/.toob/registry/versions/main /root/.toob/registry/current

if [ "$MODE" = "pr" ] || [ "$MODE" = "matrix" ]; then
    # CI-Test Mode: Create a dummy project from the registry template
    cd /tmp && rm -rf ci-test
    mkdir -p ci-test
    cd ci-test
    cp /root/.toob/registry/versions/main/chips/$CHIP/template_device.toml device.toml

    # Inject build section with version pins from the orchestrator
    echo "" >> device.toml
    echo "[build]" >> device.toml
    echo "core_sdk = \"$CORE\"" >> device.toml
    echo "compiler = \"$COMPILER\"" >> device.toml

    if [ "$MODE" = "pr" ]; then
        # Point to the mounted Toob-Loader monorepo for unmerged PR Core SDK sources
        export TOOB_COMPILER_DIR=/workspace
        cd /workspace/cli/toob-cli

        echo "[toob-ci-build] Verifying protocol contracts (ports.go)..."
        go test ./internal/ports/ -count=1

        echo "[toob-ci-build] Enforcing ProtocolVersion bumps for Breaking Changes..."
        git fetch origin main >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
        mkdir -p /tmp/ci-test-abi
        git show origin/main:cli/toob-cli/internal/ports/ports.go > /tmp/ci-test-abi/ports_old.go 2>/dev/null || touch /tmp/ci-test-abi/ports_old.go
        go run ./internal/ports/cmd/semver /tmp/ci-test-abi/ports_old.go ./internal/ports/ports.go > /dev/null || {
            echo "[toob-ci-build] FATAL: Contract validation failed. See error above."
            exit 1
        }

        go run . build --native --manifest /tmp/ci-test/device.toml
    else
        # In 'matrix' mode, TOOB_COMPILER_DIR is not set, forcing a native GitHub checkout!
        toob build --native --manifest /tmp/ci-test/device.toml
    fi
else
    # Production Mode: User project already mounted at /workspace
    # The CLI validates completeness (device.toml, chip, etc.) before building
    cd /workspace
    toob build --native
fi

FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci/api_server.go
======================================================================

package main

import (
	"crypto/subtle"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// jsonError is a helper for returning standardized JSON error responses
func jsonError(w http.ResponseWriter, msg string, code int) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(code)
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"error": msg})
}

type VersionIndex struct {
	GeneratedAt string `json:"generated_at"`
	MainBranch  struct {
		Chips []struct {
			Name    string `json:"name"`
			Version string `json:"version"`
		} `json:"chips"`
		Integrations []struct {
			Name    string `json:"name"`
			Version string `json:"version"`
		} `json:"integrations"`
	} `json:"main_branch"`
	Official struct {
		Registry []struct {
			Version          string `json:"version"`
			Source           string `json:"source"`
			InternalSnapshot struct {
				Chips []struct {
					Name    string `json:"name"`
					Version string `json:"version"`
				} `json:"chips"`
				Integrations []struct {
					Name    string `json:"name"`
					Version string `json:"version"`
				} `json:"integrations"`
			} `json:"internal_snapshot"`
		} `json:"registry"`
	} `json:"official"`
}


type PollingManager[T any] struct {
	url        string
	backupPath string
	mu         sync.RWMutex
	items      []*T // Keep up to 3
	lastHash   string
	lastFetch  time.Time
}

func NewPollingManager[T any](url, backupPath string) *PollingManager[T] {
	m := &PollingManager[T]{
		url:        url,
		backupPath: backupPath,
		items:      make([]*T, 0, 3),
	}
	go m.fetchLoop()
	return m
}

func (m *PollingManager[T]) fetchLoop() {
	backoff := 2 * time.Second
	for {
		if m.fetch() {
			break
		}
		
		// Try to load from backup
		m.mu.RLock()
		empty := len(m.items) == 0
		m.mu.RUnlock()
		
		if empty {
			if m.loadBackup() {
				log.Printf("[API] Loaded registry index from backup")
				break
			}
			log.Printf("[API] Fetch failed, retrying in %v...", backoff)
			time.Sleep(backoff)
			backoff *= 2
			if backoff > 60*time.Second {
				backoff = 60 * time.Second
			}
		} else {
			break
		}
	}
	ticker := time.NewTicker(10 * time.Minute)
	for range ticker.C {
		m.fetch()
	}
}

func (m *PollingManager[T]) loadBackup() bool {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(m.backupPath)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	var newItem T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &newItem); err != nil {
		return false
	}
	m.mu.Lock()
	m.items = append([]*T{&newItem}, m.items...)
	m.mu.Unlock()
	return true
}

func (m *PollingManager[T]) fetch() bool {
	client := http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}
	req, err := http.NewRequest("GET", m.url, nil)
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to create request for %s: %v", m.url, err)
		return false
	}
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", "Toob-CI-Hub")
	
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to fetch %s: %v", m.url, err)
		return false
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()

	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		log.Printf("[API] Bad status from GitHub %s: %d", m.url, resp.StatusCode)
		return false
	}

	body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to read %s: %v", m.url, err)
		return false
	}

	hash := sha256.Sum256(body)
	hashStr := hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])

	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()

	if m.lastHash == hashStr {
		m.lastFetch = time.Now()
		return true // No change
	}

	var newItem T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &newItem); err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to parse %s: %v", m.url, err)
		return false
	}

	if m.backupPath != "" {
		_ = os.WriteFile(m.backupPath, body, 0644)
	}

	log.Printf("[API] Downloaded update for %s", m.url)

	m.items = append([]*T{&newItem}, m.items...)
	if len(m.items) > 3 {
		m.items = m.items[:3]
	}
	m.lastHash = hashStr
	m.lastFetch = time.Now()
	return true
}

func (m *PollingManager[T]) GetLatest() *T {
	m.mu.RLock()
	defer m.mu.RUnlock()
	if len(m.items) > 0 {
		return m.items[0]
	}
	return nil
}

type APIController struct {
	VersionIndexMgr *PollingManager[VersionIndex]
	Store           *LedgerStore

	matrixCache     *Matrix
	matrixCacheTime time.Time
	matrixCacheMu   sync.RWMutex
	
	apiLimiter      chan struct{}
}

func NewAPIController(store *LedgerStore) *APIController {
	regDir := "/repo-registry"
	if store != nil {
		regDir = filepath.Dir(store.regPath)
	}
	backupPath := filepath.Join(regDir, "version_index_backup.json")
	return &APIController{
		VersionIndexMgr: NewPollingManager[VersionIndex]("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/main/version_index.json", backupPath),
		Store:           store,
		apiLimiter:      make(chan struct{}, 100),
	}
}

func (c *APIController) Limit(next http.HandlerFunc) http.HandlerFunc {
	return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		select {
		case c.apiLimiter <- struct{}{}:
			defer func() { <-c.apiLimiter }()
			next(w, r)
		default:
			jsonError(w, "Too many requests", http.StatusTooManyRequests)
		}
	}
}

// HandleResolveRegistry serves /api/v1/resolve/registry
func (c *APIController) HandleResolveRegistry(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	version := r.URL.Query().Get("version")
	if version == "" {
		version = "latest"
	}

	idx := c.VersionIndexMgr.GetLatest()
	if idx == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Registry index not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	downloadURL := ""
	resolvedVer := version

	if version == "latest" || version == "main" {
		downloadURL = "https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/archive/refs/heads/main.zip"
		resolvedVer = "main"
	} else {
		found := false
		for _, reg := range idx.Official.Registry {
			if reg.Version == version {
				downloadURL = fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/archive/refs/tags/%s.zip", version)
				found = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !found {
			// Fallback: Assume it's a valid tag even if not in index, but warn.
			downloadURL = fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/archive/refs/tags/%s.zip", version)
		}
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"version":      resolvedVer,
		"download_url": downloadURL,
	})
}

// HandleResolveChip serves /api/v1/resolve/chip
func (c *APIController) HandleResolveChip(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	chipName := r.URL.Query().Get("name")
	chipVersion := r.URL.Query().Get("version") // Optional

	if chipName == "" {
		jsonError(w, "Missing 'name' query parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	idx := c.VersionIndexMgr.GetLatest()
	if idx == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Registry index not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	// 1. Search in main_branch first
	foundVer := "main"
	foundDownload := "https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/archive/refs/heads/main.zip"

	// 2. Search official releases (which is sorted newest first usually)
	for _, reg := range idx.Official.Registry {
		for _, chip := range reg.InternalSnapshot.Chips {
			if chip.Name == chipName {
				if chipVersion == "" || chip.Version == chipVersion {
					// We found a specific release that contains it!
					foundVer = reg.Version
					foundDownload = fmt.Sprintf("https://github.com/Toob-Boot/Toob-Registry/archive/refs/tags/%s.zip", reg.Version)
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if foundVer != "main" {
			break
		}
	}

	if foundVer == "main" {
		// Verify if it's in main regardless of chipVersion
		foundInMain := false
		for _, chip := range idx.MainBranch.Chips {
			if chip.Name == chipName {
				if chipVersion == "" || chip.Version == chipVersion {
					foundInMain = true
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if !foundInMain {
			if chipVersion == "" {
				jsonError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Chip %s not found in registry", chipName), http.StatusNotFound)
			} else {
				jsonError(w, fmt.Sprintf("Chip %s version %s not found in registry", chipName, chipVersion), http.StatusNotFound)
			}
			return
		}
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"chip":                      chipName,
		"chip_version":              chipVersion,
		"found_in_registry_version": foundVer,
		"registry_download_url":     foundDownload,
	})
}

// HandleResolveIntegrations serves /api/v1/resolve/integrations
func (c *APIController) HandleResolveIntegrations(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	idx := c.VersionIndexMgr.GetLatest()
	if idx == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Registry index not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	
	type integrationResponse struct {
		Name    string `json:"name"`
		Version string `json:"version"`
	}

	intMap := make(map[string]string)

	// Try Official Registry releases first (newest to oldest)
	for _, reg := range idx.Official.Registry {
		for _, intg := range reg.InternalSnapshot.Integrations {
			if _, exists := intMap[intg.Name]; !exists {
				intMap[intg.Name] = intg.Version
			}
		}
	}
	// Fallback to Main Branch for unreleased ones
	for _, intg := range idx.MainBranch.Integrations {
		if _, exists := intMap[intg.Name]; !exists {
			intMap[intg.Name] = intg.Version
		}
	}

	var results []integrationResponse
	for name, ver := range intMap {
		results = append(results, integrationResponse{
			Name:    name,
			Version: ver,
		})
	}

	// Make sure we never return null for the array
	if results == nil {
		results = []integrationResponse{}
	}

	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"integrations": results,
	})
}

func (c *APIController) HandleResolveEnvironment(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	chipName := r.URL.Query().Get("chip")
	chipVersion := r.URL.Query().Get("chip_version")
	cliVersion := r.URL.Query().Get("cli_version")

	if chipName == "" || cliVersion == "" {
		jsonError(w, "Missing 'chip' or 'cli_version' parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(cliVersion, "v") && cliVersion != "latest" && cliVersion != "main" && cliVersion != "dev" {
		cliVersion = "v" + cliVersion
	}

	if c.Store == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Store not initialized", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	// Resolve "latest" CLI Version to an actual version from the registry index
	if cliVersion == "latest" {
		idx := c.VersionIndexMgr.GetLatest()
		if idx != nil && len(idx.Official.Registry) > 0 {
			cliVersion = idx.Official.Registry[0].Version
		}
	}

	res, err := c.Store.ResolveEnvironment(chipName, cliVersion, chipVersion)
	if err != nil {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"error": fmt.Sprintf("Database error: %v", err)})
		return
	}
	if res == nil {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
			"status":               "UNKNOWN",
			"recommended_compiler": "latest",
			"recommended_core_sdk": "main",
			"error":                "Chip not found or no compatible environment",
		})
		return
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"status":               res.Status,
		"recommended_compiler": res.RecommendedCompiler,
		"recommended_core_sdk": res.RecommendedCoreSDK,
		"chip_version_used":    res.ChipVersionUsed,
	})
}

// HandleResolveMatrix serves /api/v1/resolve/matrix
// Returns the full compatibility matrix from SQLite in CLI-compatible shape.
// Optional ?chip= filter for single-chip lookups.
func (c *APIController) HandleResolveMatrix(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	if c.Store == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Store not initialized", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	chipFilter := r.URL.Query().Get("chip")

	// Use TTL cache for matrix lookups
	c.matrixCacheMu.RLock()
	if c.matrixCache != nil && time.Since(c.matrixCacheTime) < 30*time.Second {
		cached := c.matrixCache
		c.matrixCacheMu.RUnlock()
		
		// If chipFilter is present, filter the cached matrix instead of bypassing to DB
		if chipFilter != "" {
			filtered := make(Matrix)
			if chipData, ok := (*cached)[chipFilter]; ok {
				filtered[chipFilter] = chipData
			}
			w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
			if err := json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(filtered); err != nil {
				log.Printf("[API] Failed to encode filtered matrix: %v", err)
			}
			return
		}

		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		if err := json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(cached); err != nil {
			log.Printf("[API] Failed to encode cached matrix: %v", err)
		}
		return
	}
	c.matrixCacheMu.RUnlock()

	matrix, err := c.Store.QueryMatrix(chipFilter)
	if err != nil {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"error": fmt.Sprintf("Database error: %v", err)})
		return
	}

	if chipFilter == "" {
		c.matrixCacheMu.Lock()
		c.matrixCache = matrix
		c.matrixCacheTime = time.Now()
		c.matrixCacheMu.Unlock()
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	if err := json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(matrix); err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to encode matrix response: %v", err)
	}
}

// HandleResolveCombination serves /api/v1/resolve/combination
// Binary go/no-go validation for a specific tuple.
// Required: chip. Optional: chip_version, cli, core, compiler.
func (c *APIController) HandleResolveCombination(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}
	chip := r.URL.Query().Get("chip")
	if chip == "" {
		jsonError(w, "Missing required 'chip' parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	if c.Store == nil {
		jsonError(w, "Store not initialized", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
		return
	}

	chipVersion := r.URL.Query().Get("chip_version")
	cli := r.URL.Query().Get("cli")
	core := r.URL.Query().Get("core")
	compiler := r.URL.Query().Get("compiler")

	result, err := c.Store.QueryCombination(chip, chipVersion, cli, core, compiler)
	if err != nil {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"error": fmt.Sprintf("Database error: %v", err)})
		return
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(result)
}

// HandleMatrixTrigger serves /api/v1/admin/matrix-trigger
func (c *APIController) HandleMatrixTrigger(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		jsonError(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}

	secret := os.Getenv("WEBHOOK_SECRET")
	if secret != "" {
		auth := r.Header.Get("Authorization")
		expectedAuth := "Bearer " + secret
		if subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(auth), []byte(expectedAuth)) != 1 {
			jsonError(w, "Unauthorized", http.StatusUnauthorized)
			return
		}
	}

	// Concurrency-safe SQLite operation
	cleared, err := c.Store.ClearUnverifiedEntries()
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("[API] Failed to clear unverified entries in ledger.db: %v", err)
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"error": "Failed to access ledger.db"})
		return
	}

	// Wake up Matrix Poller
	select {
	case ForceMatrixWakeup <- struct{}{}:
	default:
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
		"status": "success",
		"cleared_errors": cleared,
	})
}

FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci/assertions_test.go

package main

import (
	"reflect"
	"testing"
)

// TestPortFieldParity verifies that fields shared between a port contract
// type and its implementation counterpart have matching Go types.
//
// Port types define the external protocol (webhook JSON, Docker API).
// Impl types are the internal representation after transformation.
// They intentionally differ in field count (impl may add internal fields
// like RawEvent, or ports may define external-only fields like Action).
// This test ensures the SHARED fields don't silently diverge in type.
func TestPortFieldParity(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name     string
		portType reflect.Type
		realType reflect.Type
	}{
		{"WebhookReleasePayload ↔ ReleasePayload",
			reflect.TypeOf(WebhookReleasePayload{}), reflect.TypeOf(ReleasePayload{})},
		{"WebhookPRPayload ↔ PRCheckPayload",
			reflect.TypeOf(WebhookPRPayload{}), reflect.TypeOf(PRCheckPayload{})},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			for i := 0; i < tt.portType.NumField(); i++ {
				pf := tt.portType.Field(i)
				rf, found := tt.realType.FieldByName(pf.Name)
				if !found {
					continue // Different abstraction levels — not all fields overlap
				}
				if pf.Type != rf.Type {
					t.Errorf("\n[BREAKING CHANGE] %s: Shared field %q type diverged.\n"+
						"  Port expects %s, but implementation has %s.",
						tt.name, pf.Name, pf.Type, rf.Type)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

// TestSelfContainedPortsHaveTags ensures every exported field in every
// port struct carries a valid port:"required" or port:"optional" tag.
// This is the compile-time-equivalent lint for the Orchestrator.
func TestSelfContainedPortsHaveTags(t *testing.T) {
	portTypes := []struct {
		name string
		typ  reflect.Type
	}{
		{"SessionStartInput", reflect.TypeOf(SessionStartInput{})},
		{"SessionExecInput", reflect.TypeOf(SessionExecInput{})},
		{"SessionExecOutput", reflect.TypeOf(SessionExecOutput{})},
		{"SessionLogsOutput", reflect.TypeOf(SessionLogsOutput{})},
		{"GitHubStatusReport", reflect.TypeOf(GitHubStatusReport{})},
		{"VersionIndexFetch", reflect.TypeOf(VersionIndexFetch{})},
		{"WebhookReleasePayload", reflect.TypeOf(WebhookReleasePayload{})},
		{"WebhookPRPayload", reflect.TypeOf(WebhookPRPayload{})},
		{"MatrixResultFile", reflect.TypeOf(MatrixResultFile{})},
		{"InternalStateEntry", reflect.TypeOf(InternalStateEntry{})},
		{"RegistryPushOperation", reflect.TypeOf(RegistryPushOperation{})},
		{"CLIReleasePipeline", reflect.TypeOf(CLIReleasePipeline{})},
		{"CoreReleasePipeline", reflect.TypeOf(CoreReleasePipeline{})},
		{"CompilerReleasePipeline", reflect.TypeOf(CompilerReleasePipeline{})},
		{"TopologyTrigger", reflect.TypeOf(TopologyTrigger{})},
		{"HubResolveRegistryRequest", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveRegistryRequest{})},
		{"HubResolveRegistryResponse", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveRegistryResponse{})},
		{"HubResolveChipRequest", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveChipRequest{})},
		{"HubResolveChipResponse", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveChipResponse{})},
		{"HubResolveEnvironmentRequest", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveEnvironmentRequest{})},
		{"HubResolveEnvironmentResponse", reflect.TypeOf(HubResolveEnvironmentResponse{})},
		{"HubRetryErrorsResponse", reflect.TypeOf(HubRetryErrorsResponse{})},
		{"DaemonConfig", reflect.TypeOf(DaemonConfig{})},
		{"CompilerImageLabels", reflect.TypeOf(CompilerImageLabels{})},
	}

	for _, pt := range portTypes {
		t.Run(pt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			for i := 0; i < pt.typ.NumField(); i++ {
				f := pt.typ.Field(i)
				if !f.IsExported() {
					continue
				}
				tag := f.Tag.Get("port")
				if tag == "" {
					t.Errorf("\n[MISSING TAG] %s.%s has no port tag.\n"+
						"  -> Add `port:\"required\"` or `port:\"optional\"`.",
						pt.name, f.Name)
				} else if tag != "required" && tag != "optional" {
					t.Errorf("\n[INVALID TAG] %s.%s has port tag %q.\n"+
						"  -> Must be \"required\" or \"optional\".",
						pt.name, f.Name, tag)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

                        <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">Sync: <span id="countdown" class="text-purple-300 font-mono" data-next="{{.NextCommit}}">--:--</span></span>
                        <span class="bg-purple-900 text-purple-300 px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-bold">{{len .Outbox}}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <ul class="space-y-2 text-sm font-mono text-slate-300 h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-2">
                    {{if .Outbox}}
                        {{range .Outbox}}
                        <li class="bg-slate-800 p-2 rounded border border-slate-700 flex flex-col gap-1">

                                {{else}}
                                    <span class="text-rose-400 text-xs px-2 py-1 bg-rose-400/10 rounded">{{.Status}}</span>
                                {{end}}
                            </div>
                            <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">

                            </div>
                        </li>
                        {{end}}
                    {{else}}
                        <li class="text-slate-500 italic text-center py-4">Outbox is empty.</li>
                    {{end}}
                </ul>
            </div>

        </div>

        <div class="card">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-slate-200 border-b border-slate-600 pb-2">State Matrix (Ledger)</h2>

                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">CLI</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">Vendor</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">Toolchain</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">Compiler</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">Core</th>
                            <th class="py-2 px-3 font-medium">Result</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody class="font-mono text-xs text-slate-300">
                        {{range .Ledger}}
                        <tr class="border-b border-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition-colors">
                            <td class="py-2 px-3 text-slate-500">{{.Index}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3 text-purple-400 font-bold">{{.ID}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.Arch}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3 text-sky-300">{{.Chip}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3 text-teal-300">{{.ChipVersion}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.CLI}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.Vendor}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.Toolchain}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.Compiler}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">{{.Core}}</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-3">
                                {{if eq .Result "VERIFIED"}}
                                    <span class="text-emerald-400 px-2 py-1 bg-emerald-400/10 rounded">{{.Result}}</span>
                                {{else}}
                                    <span class="text-rose-400 px-2 py-1 bg-rose-400/10 rounded">{{.Result}}</span>
                                {{end}}
                                {{if eq .Source "active"}}
                                    {{if gt .RetryCount 0}}
                                        <span class="text-xs text-amber-400 ml-1">(Retry: {{.RetryCount}})</span>
                                    {{end}}
                                {{end}}
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        {{end}}
                        {{if not .Ledger}}
                        <tr><td colspan="11" class="py-8 text-center text-slate-500 italic">No ledger entries found.</td></tr>
                        {{end}}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    <script>
        const el = document.getElementById("countdown");
        if(el) {
            const nextUnix = parseInt(el.getAttribute("data-next"), 10);
            function update() {
                const diff = Math.floor(nextUnix - (Date.now() / 1000));
                if (diff <= 0) {
                    el.innerText = "Syncing...";
                    return;
                }
                const m = Math.floor(diff / 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
                const s = (diff % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
                el.innerText = m + ":" + s;
            }
            setInterval(update, 1000);
            update();
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
`

// dashboardCache holds pre-computed I/O-heavy data, refreshed by a background ticker.
// The HTTP handler only reads from this cache — zero filesystem or subprocess I/O per request.
type dashboardCache struct {
	mu          sync.RWMutex
	dockerStats []DockerStat
	outbox      []ResultFile
	ledger      []LedgerRow
}

var dbCache = &dashboardCache{}
var dashboardTmpl = template.Must(template.New("dashboard").Parse(dashboardHTML))

// StartDashboardCache launches a background goroutine that refreshes the
// I/O-heavy dashboard data (docker stats, outbox files, ledger) every 5 seconds.
func StartDashboardCache(store *LedgerStore) {
	refreshDashboardCache(store) // Populate immediately at startup
	go func() {
		ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Second)
		defer ticker.Stop()
		for range ticker.C {
			refreshDashboardCache(store)
		}
	}()
}

func refreshDashboardCache(store *LedgerStore) {
	if store == nil {
		return
	}
	// 1. Docker Stats (subprocess with timeout to prevent goroutine freeze)
	statsCtx, statsCancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 4*time.Second)
	defer statsCancel()
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(statsCtx, "docker", "stats", "--no-stream", "--format", `{"Name":"{{.Name}}","MemUsage":"{{.MemUsage}}","CPUPerc":"{{.CPUPerc}}"}`)
	out, _ := cmd.Output()

	var dockerStats []DockerStat
	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), "\n")
	for _, line := range lines {
		if line == "" {
			continue
		}
		var stat DockerStat
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &stat); err == nil {
			stat.Name = strings.TrimPrefix(stat.Name, "/")
			if strings.Contains(stat.Name, "act-") {
				stat.Name = "release-runner (act)"
			}
			dockerStats = append(dockerStats, stat)
		}
	}

	// 2. Outbox files
	var outbox []ResultFile
	files, _ := filepath.Glob("/repo-registry/result_*.json")
	for _, f := range files {
		data, err := os.ReadFile(f)
		if err == nil {
			var res struct {
				Chip            string `json:"chip"`
				ChipVersion     string `json:"chip_version"`
				CliVersion      string `json:"cli_version"`
				CoreVersion     string `json:"core_version"`
				CompilerVersion string `json:"compiler_version"`
				Status          string `json:"status"`
			}
			if json.Unmarshal(data, &res) == nil {
				jobID := GenerateComboID("MT", res.Chip, res.ChipVersion, res.CliVersion, res.CoreVersion, res.CompilerVersion)
				outbox = append(outbox, ResultFile{
					Name:        filepath.Base(f),
					ID:          jobID,
					Type:        "MT",
					Chip:        res.Chip,
					ChipVersion: res.ChipVersion,
					CLI:         res.CliVersion,
					Core:        res.CoreVersion,
					Compiler:    res.CompilerVersion,
					Status:      res.Status,
				})
			}
		}
	}

	// 3. Ledger from SQLite
	ledger, err := store.QueryDashboardRows()
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Printf("[Dashboard] Error querying SQLite ledger: %v\n", err)
	}

	// Populate Registry Metadata
	reg := store.GetRegistry()
	if reg != nil {
		for i := range ledger {
			ledger[i].Index = i + 1
			if chipMeta, ok := reg.Chips[ledger[i].Chip]; ok {
				ledger[i].Arch = chipMeta.Arch
				ledger[i].Vendor = chipMeta.Vendor
				tcName := chipMeta.CompilerPrefix
				if len(tcName) > 0 && tcName[len(tcName)-1] == '-' {
					tcName = tcName[:len(tcName)-1]
				}
				ledger[i].Toolchain = tcName
			} else {
				ledger[i].Arch = "unknown"
				ledger[i].Vendor = "unknown"
				ledger[i].Toolchain = "unknown"
			}
		}
	}

	// Atomic swap
	dbCache.mu.Lock()
	dbCache.dockerStats = dockerStats
	dbCache.outbox = outbox
	if err == nil {
		dbCache.ledger = ledger
	}
	dbCache.mu.Unlock()
}

func (ws *WebhookServer) HandleDashboard(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	status := ws.Planner.GetStatus()

	// Read pre-computed I/O data from background cache
	dbCache.mu.RLock()
	dockerStats := dbCache.dockerStats
	outbox := dbCache.outbox
	ledger := dbCache.ledger
	dbCache.mu.RUnlock()

	var inboxSlice []map[string]interface{}
	if val, ok := status["inbox"].([]map[string]interface{}); ok {
		inboxSlice = val
	}

	var queueSlice []map[string]interface{}
	if val, ok := status["queue"].([]map[string]interface{}); ok {
		queueSlice = val
	}

	var slotSlice []map[string]interface{}
	if val, ok := status["slots"].([]map[string]interface{}); ok {
		slotSlice = val
	}

	var nextCommit int64
	if val, ok := status["next_commit"].(int64); ok {
		nextCommit = val
	}

	inboxSize := 0
	if val, ok := status["inbox_size"].(int); ok {
		inboxSize = val
	}

	data := DashboardData{
		PlannerState: fmt.Sprintf("%v", status["planner_state"]),
		InboxSize:    inboxSize,
		Inbox:        inboxSlice,
		Queue:        queueSlice,
		Slots:        slotSlice,
		DockerStats:  dockerStats,
		Outbox:       outbox,
		Ledger:       ledger,
		NextCommit:   nextCommit,
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/html")
	dashboardTmpl.Execute(w, data)
}

package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"syscall"
	"time"
)

func main() {
	log.Println("=== Starting Toob-CI Unified Daemon ===")

	webhookSecret := os.Getenv("WEBHOOK_SECRET")
	if webhookSecret == "" {
		log.Fatal("FATAL: WEBHOOK_SECRET environment variable is missing")
	}

	// Ensure log directory exists
	os.MkdirAll("/cache/logs", 0755)

	workerCount := 2
	if wcStr := os.Getenv("WORKER_COUNT"); wcStr != "" {
		if val, err := strconv.Atoi(wcStr); err == nil && val > 0 {
			workerCount = val
		}
	}

	// Initialize GlobalStore before planner starts
	var err error
	GlobalStore, err = NewLedgerStore("/repo-registry")
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("WARNING: Failed to init GlobalStore: %v", err)
	} else {
		defer GlobalStore.Close()
	}

	planner := NewPlanner(workerCount)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	planner.Start(&wg)

	// Start Background Matrix Poller
	go StartMatrixPoller(planner)

	// Start Background Ledger Committer
	go StartLedgerCommitter(planner)

	// Start Dashboard I/O Cache (async refresh every 5s)
	StartDashboardCache(GlobalStore)

	// Start Webhook Listener
	server := &WebhookServer{
		Planner: planner,
		Secret:  webhookSecret,
	}

	http.HandleFunc("/", server.HandleDashboard)
	http.HandleFunc("/hooks/release", server.HandleRelease)
	http.HandleFunc("/hooks/pull_request", server.HandlePR)
	
	// Expose Logs
	http.Handle("/logs/", http.StripPrefix("/logs/", http.FileServer(http.Dir("/cache/logs"))))

	// Toob Hub API (Interpreter Service)
	apiManager := NewAPIController(GlobalStore)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/registry", apiManager.HandleResolveRegistry)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/chip", apiManager.HandleResolveChip)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/environment", apiManager.HandleResolveEnvironment)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/integrations", apiManager.HandleResolveIntegrations)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/matrix", apiManager.HandleResolveMatrix)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/resolve/combination", apiManager.HandleResolveCombination)
	http.HandleFunc("/api/v1/admin/matrix-trigger", apiManager.HandleMatrixTrigger)

	// Gap #9: Health Check Endpoint
	http.HandleFunc("/health", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
		w.Write([]byte("ok"))
	})

	// Status endpoint for run tracking and queue sizes
	http.HandleFunc("/status", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		status := planner.GetStatus()
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(status)
	})

	port := os.Getenv("PORT")
	if port == "" {
		port = "9000"
	}

	// Gap #12: Graceful Shutdown
	sigCh := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
	signal.Notify(sigCh, syscall.SIGTERM, syscall.SIGINT)
	go func() {
		sig := <-sigCh
		log.Printf("[Daemon] Received %s. Waiting for current jobs to finish...", sig)
		planner.Shutdown()
		wg.Wait()
		if GlobalStore != nil {
			GlobalStore.Close()
		}
		log.Println("[Daemon] All sessions closed. Exiting.")
		os.Exit(0)
	}()

	log.Printf("[Daemon] Webhook Server listening on port %s", port)
	
	srv := &http.Server{
		Addr:              ":" + port,
		Handler:           nil,
		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		ReadTimeout:       10 * time.Second,
		WriteTimeout:      30 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("FATAL: HTTP Server failed: %v", err)
	}
}

FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci/matrix.go

package main

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const maxQueueBatch = 500

var ForceMatrixWakeup = make(chan struct{}, 1)

func StartMatrixPoller(p *Planner) {
	for {
		queue, regVersion := generateMatrixQueue()
		if len(queue) == 0 {
			log.Println("[MatrixPoller] No targets to test. Sleeping for 1 hour...")
			select {
			case <-time.After(1 * time.Hour):
			case <-ForceMatrixWakeup:
				log.Println("[MatrixPoller] Wakeup signal received!")
			}
			continue
		}

		log.Printf("[MatrixPoller] Found %d targets in the queue. Grouping by Compiler for Session optimization...", len(queue))
		
		runID := fmt.Sprintf("nightly-%d", time.Now().Unix())

		// Group by Compiler to maximize Session reuse (Compiler-Keeper logic)
		packagesByCompiler := make(map[string]*WorkPackage)
		
		for _, target := range queue {
			wp, exists := packagesByCompiler[target.Compiler]
			if !exists {
				wp = &WorkPackage{
					ID:              fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", runID, target.Compiler),
					Priority:        2, // Low Priority
					Type:            JobTypeMatrix,
					CompilerTag:     target.Compiler,
					RegistryVersion: int(regVersion),
					CreatedAt:       time.Now().Unix(),
					Jobs:            []Job{},
				}
				packagesByCompiler[target.Compiler] = wp
			}
			
			wp.Jobs = append(wp.Jobs, Job{
				ID:      GenerateComboID("MT", target.Chip, target.ChipVersion, target.Cli, target.Core, target.Compiler),
				Type:    JobTypeMatrix,
				RunID:   runID,
				Payload: target,
			})
		}

		// Submit all packages to the planner
		for _, wp := range packagesByCompiler {
			p.Submit(wp)
		}

		log.Printf("[MatrixPoller] Submitted %d Compiler WorkPackages to the planner. Waiting 1 hour before polling again...", len(packagesByCompiler))
		select {
		case <-time.After(1 * time.Hour):
		case <-ForceMatrixWakeup:
			log.Println("[MatrixPoller] Wakeup signal received!")
		}
	}
}

func generateMatrixQueue() ([]MatrixTarget, int64) {
	cmd := exec.Command("toob-matrix-generator")
	cmd.Dir = "/repo-registry"
	
	out, err := cmd.Output()
	if err != nil {
		log.Printf("[MatrixPoller] Failed to generate matrix: %v", err)
		return nil, 0
	}

	var queue []MatrixTarget
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &queue); err != nil {
		log.Printf("[MatrixPoller] Failed to parse matrix output: %v", err)
		return nil, 0
	}

	// Parse registry_version for priority sorting
	var regVersion int64
	regBytes, err := os.ReadFile("/repo-registry/registry.json")
	if err == nil {
		var reg map[string]interface{}
		if json.Unmarshal(regBytes, &reg) == nil {
			verStr, _ := reg["registry_version"].(string)
			if verStr == "" {
				verStr = "v0.0.0"
			}
			ver := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(verStr, "v"))
			parts := strings.Split(ver, ".")
			if len(parts) == 3 {
				major, _ := strconv.ParseInt(parts[0], 10, 64)
				minor, _ := strconv.ParseInt(parts[1], 10, 64)
				patch, _ := strconv.ParseInt(parts[2], 10, 64)
				regVersion = major*10000 + minor*100 + patch
			}
		}
	}
	return queue, regVersion
}


======================================================================
FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci/planner.go
======================================================================

package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"hash/crc32"
	"log"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

type Planner struct {
	State        PlannerState
	Packages     []*WorkPackage
	inbox        []*WorkPackage
	mu           sync.Mutex
	cond         *sync.Cond
	planningLock bool
	slots        []*Slot
	shuttingDown bool

	runs          map[string]*Run
	matrixBatches int
	NextLedgerCommit int64

	recentJobs    map[string]int64
	recentJobsMu  sync.Mutex
	activePulls   map[string]bool
	activePullsMu sync.Mutex

	rootCtx    context.Context
	rootCancel context.CancelFunc
}

type Slot struct {
	ID          int
	State       SlotState
	ActiveJob   *WorkPackage
	CurrentTask map[string]string
}

func GenerateComboID(prefix, chip, chipVersion, cli, core, compiler string) string {
	str := fmt.Sprintf("chip=%s@%s::cli=%s::core=%s::compiler=%s", chip, chipVersion, cli, core, compiler)
	h := crc32.ChecksumIEEE([]byte(str))
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s-%08X", prefix, h)
}

func NewPlanner(slotCount int) *Planner {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	p := &Planner{
		State:    PlannerStateCollecting,
		Packages: make([]*WorkPackage, 0),
		inbox:    make([]*WorkPackage, 0),
		runs:     make(map[string]*Run),
		recentJobs:  make(map[string]int64),
		activePulls: make(map[string]bool),
		rootCtx:  ctx,
		rootCancel: cancel,
	}
	p.cond = sync.NewCond(&p.mu)

	for i := 0; i < slotCount; i++ {
		p.slots = append(p.slots, &Slot{ID: i, State: SlotStateIdle})
	}
	return p
}

func (p *Planner) Submit(wp *WorkPackage) {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	p.inbox = append(p.inbox, wp)
	p.cond.Broadcast() // Wake up planner
}

func (p *Planner) Start(wg *sync.WaitGroup) {
	go p.planningLoop()

	for _, slot := range p.slots {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(s *Slot) {
			defer wg.Done()
			p.slotRunner(s)
		}(slot)
	}
}

func (p *Planner) Shutdown() {
	p.mu.Lock()
	p.shuttingDown = true
	p.rootCancel()
	p.cond.Broadcast()
	p.mu.Unlock()
}

func (p *Planner) planningLoop() {
	for {
		p.mu.Lock()
		for len(p.inbox) == 0 && !p.shuttingDown {
			p.cond.Wait()
		}
		if p.shuttingDown {
			p.mu.Unlock()
			return
		}

		// Drain the inbox
		currentInbox := p.inbox
		p.inbox = make([]*WorkPackage, 0)

		// Enter PLANNING state
		p.State = PlannerStatePlanning
		p.planningLock = true // Tell slots to wait before taking next package
		p.mu.Unlock()

		log.Printf("[Planner] Trigger received. Draining %d items from Inbox and re-calculating execution plan...", len(currentInbox))

		var newPackages []*WorkPackage
		for _, wp := range currentInbox {
			if wp.Type == JobTypePRCheck && len(wp.Jobs) == 1 && wp.Jobs[0].ID == "raw-pr-event" {
				// Expand PR Test Plan
				expanded := p.buildPRTestPlan(wp)
				newPackages = append(newPackages, expanded...)
			} else {
				newPackages = append(newPackages, wp)
			}
		}

		p.mu.Lock()
		p.Packages = append(p.Packages, newPackages...)

		// Job Deduplication (Gap 5.1 & Gap 1.2 Fix)
		p.recentJobsMu.Lock()
		
		// GC old jobs (> 1 hour)
		now := time.Now().Unix()
		for k, ts := range p.recentJobs {
			if now-ts > 3600 {
				delete(p.recentJobs, k)
			}
		}

		var deduplicatedPackages []*WorkPackage

		for _, wp := range p.Packages {
			var keepJobs []Job
			for _, job := range wp.Jobs {
				dedupKey := job.ID
				if wp.Type == JobTypePRCheck {
					if prData, ok := job.Payload.(PRCheckPayload); ok {
						dedupKey = fmt.Sprintf("%s-%s", job.ID, prData.Sha)
					}
				}
				
				if _, exists := p.recentJobs[dedupKey]; !exists {
					p.recentJobs[dedupKey] = now
					keepJobs = append(keepJobs, job)
				} else {
					log.Printf("[Planner] Dropping duplicate job: %s", dedupKey)
					// Decrement TotalJobs in p.runs to avoid hanging runs
					runID := job.RunID
					if runID == "" {
						runID = wp.ID
					}
					if run, exists := p.runs[runID]; exists {
						run.TotalJobs--
						if run.TotalJobs == 0 {
							delete(p.runs, runID)
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if len(keepJobs) > 0 {
				wp.Jobs = keepJobs
				deduplicatedPackages = append(deduplicatedPackages, wp)
			}
		}
		p.Packages = deduplicatedPackages
		p.recentJobsMu.Unlock()

		// Queue Prioritization Algorithm
		sort.Slice(p.Packages, func(i, j int) bool {
			// 1. Package Priority (High = 0, Medium = 1, Low = 2)
			if p.Packages[i].Priority != p.Packages[j].Priority {
				return p.Packages[i].Priority < p.Packages[j].Priority
			}

			// 2. Matrix Specific Sorting (Registry Version Oldest First, then Compiler)
			if p.Packages[i].Priority == 2 {
				if p.Packages[i].RegistryVersion != p.Packages[j].RegistryVersion {
					return p.Packages[i].RegistryVersion < p.Packages[j].RegistryVersion
				}
				if p.Packages[i].CompilerTag != p.Packages[j].CompilerTag {
					return p.Packages[i].CompilerTag < p.Packages[j].CompilerTag
				}
			}

			// 3. FIFO
			return p.Packages[i].CreatedAt < p.Packages[j].CreatedAt
		})

		// Cleanup empty packages
		var active []*WorkPackage
		for _, wp := range p.Packages {
			if len(wp.Jobs) > 0 {
				active = append(active, wp)
			}
		}
		p.Packages = active

		// Docker Image Precache (Gap 5.2 & Gap 2.1 Fix)
		go func(packages []*WorkPackage) {
			for _, wp := range packages {
				if wp.CompilerTag == "" {
					continue
				}
				img := fmt.Sprintf("mannomannx/toob-compiler:%s", wp.CompilerTag)
				
				p.activePullsMu.Lock()
				if p.activePulls[img] {
					p.activePullsMu.Unlock()
					continue
				}
				p.activePulls[img] = true
				p.activePullsMu.Unlock()

				// Check if image exists locally first
				if err := exec.Command("docker", "image", "inspect", img).Run(); err != nil {
					log.Printf("[Planner] Precaching Docker image in background: %s", img)
					cmd := exec.CommandContext(p.rootCtx, "docker", "pull", img)
					cmd.Run()
				}

				p.activePullsMu.Lock()
				delete(p.activePulls, img)
				p.activePullsMu.Unlock()
			}
		}(active)

		p.State = PlannerStateExecuting
		p.planningLock = false // Release lock
		p.cond.Broadcast()     // Wake up slots
		p.mu.Unlock()

		// If inbox is empty, it waits on cond.Wait() synchronously.
	}
}

func (p *Planner) popNextPackage() *WorkPackage {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()

	for (p.planningLock || len(p.Packages) == 0) && !p.shuttingDown {
		p.cond.Wait()
	}

	if p.shuttingDown || len(p.Packages) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	wp := p.Packages[0]
	p.Packages[0] = nil // Avoid memory leak
	p.Packages = p.Packages[1:]
	return wp
}

func (p *Planner) slotRunner(slot *Slot) {
	for {
		wp := p.popNextPackage()
		if wp == nil {
			if p.shuttingDown {
				return
			}
			time.Sleep(1 * time.Second)
			continue
		}

		p.mu.Lock()
		slot.ActiveJob = wp
		p.mu.Unlock()

		switch wp.Type {
		case JobTypeRelease:
			job := wp.Jobs[0]
			releaseData := job.Payload.(ReleasePayload)

			p.mu.Lock()
			slot.State = SlotStateRunningRelease
			slot.CurrentTask = map[string]string{
				"id":  job.ID,
				"tag": releaseData.Ref,
			}
			p.mu.Unlock()

			executeReleaseJob(slot.ID, releaseData)

		case JobTypePRCheck, JobTypeMatrix:
			p.mu.Lock()
			slot.State = SlotStateRunningSession
			p.mu.Unlock()
			secMode := "PR"
			if wp.Type == JobTypeMatrix {
				secMode = "MATRIX"
			}

			var prRepo, prSha string
			if wp.Type == JobTypePRCheck && len(wp.Jobs) > 0 {
				if prData, ok := wp.Jobs[0].Payload.(PRCheckPayload); ok {
					prRepo = prData.RepoName
					prSha = prData.Sha
				}
			}

			session := &CompilerSession{
				ContainerName: fmt.Sprintf("slot-%d-compiler", slot.ID),
				CompilerTag:   wp.CompilerTag,
				SecurityMode:  secMode,
				PRRepo:        prRepo,
				PRSha:         prSha,
			}

			// Scale timeout: base 30min + 5min per job, capped at 4h
			timeout := 30*time.Minute + time.Duration(len(wp.Jobs))*5*time.Minute
			if timeout > 4*time.Hour {
				timeout = 4 * time.Hour
			}
			sessionCtx, sessionCancel := context.WithTimeout(p.rootCtx, timeout)

			if err := session.Start(sessionCtx); err != nil {
				log.Printf("[Slot %d] FATAL: Failed to start session %s: %v", slot.ID, session.ContainerName, err)
				sessionCancel()
				
				if wp.Type == JobTypePRCheck && len(wp.Jobs) > 0 {
					if prData, ok := wp.Jobs[0].Payload.(PRCheckPayload); ok {
						reportFailureToGitHub(prData.RepoName, prData.Sha)
					}
				}
				
				runID := wp.ID
				if len(wp.Jobs) > 0 && wp.Jobs[0].RunID != "" {
				    runID = wp.Jobs[0].RunID
				}
				p.mu.Lock()
				delete(p.runs, runID)
				p.mu.Unlock()

				continue
			}

			for i, job := range wp.Jobs {
				// Per-job timeout prevents a single slow build from starving the rest
				jobCtx, jobCancel := context.WithTimeout(sessionCtx, 5*time.Minute)

				if wp.Type == JobTypePRCheck {
					prData := job.Payload.(PRCheckPayload)
					p.mu.Lock()
					slot.CurrentTask = map[string]string{
						"id":       job.ID,
						"chip":     prData.Chips[0],
						"cli":      "main",
						"core":     prData.CoreVersion,
						"compiler": prData.CompilerVersion,
					}
					p.mu.Unlock()
					output, err := session.Exec(jobCtx, prData.Chips[0], prData.CoreVersion, prData.CompilerVersion)
					if err != nil {
						log.Printf("[Slot %d] PR #%d FAILED for chip %s: %s", slot.ID, prData.PRNumber, prData.Chips[0], string(output))
						reportFailureToGitHub(prData.RepoName, prData.Sha)
						
						runID := job.RunID
						if runID == "" {
							runID = wp.ID
						}
						p.mu.Lock()
						if run, exists := p.runs[runID]; exists {
							remaining := len(wp.Jobs) - i
							run.Failed += int32(remaining)
							run.Completed += int32(remaining)
							if run.Completed == int32(run.TotalJobs) {
								delete(p.runs, runID)
							}
						}
						p.mu.Unlock()
						jobCancel()
						break
					} else {
						log.Printf("[Slot %d] PR #%d SUCCESS for chip %s.", slot.ID, prData.PRNumber, prData.Chips[0])
						runID := job.RunID
						if runID == "" {
							runID = wp.ID
						}
						var shouldReportSuccess bool
						p.mu.Lock()
						if run, exists := p.runs[runID]; exists {
							run.Completed++
							if run.Completed == int32(run.TotalJobs) {
								if run.Failed == 0 {
									log.Printf("[Slot %d] PR Run %s COMPLETE! All jobs succeeded.", slot.ID, runID)
									shouldReportSuccess = true
								}
								delete(p.runs, runID)
							}
						}
						p.mu.Unlock()
						
						if shouldReportSuccess {
							reportSuccessToGitHub(prData.RepoName, prData.Sha)
						}
					}
				} else if wp.Type == JobTypeMatrix {
					matData := job.Payload.(MatrixTarget)
					p.mu.Lock()
					slot.CurrentTask = map[string]string{
						"id":           job.ID,
						"chip":         matData.Chip,
						"chip_version": matData.ChipVersion,
						"cli":          matData.Cli,
						"core":         matData.Core,
						"compiler":     matData.Compiler,
					}
					p.mu.Unlock()
					output, err := session.Exec(jobCtx, matData.Chip, matData.Core, matData.Compiler)

					status := "VERIFIED"
					if err != nil {
						status = extractErrorStatus(output)
						log.Printf("[Slot %d] Matrix FAILED chip=%s: %s", slot.ID, matData.Chip, string(output))
					}
					writeMatrixResult(matData, status)

					p.mu.Lock()
					p.matrixBatches++
					if p.matrixBatches >= 50 {
						select {
						case ForceLedgerCommit <- struct{}{}:
						default:
						}
					}
					p.mu.Unlock()
				}
				jobCancel()
			}

			session.Stop()
			sessionCancel()

			// Trigger ledger commit when a matrix package finishes
			if wp.Type == JobTypeMatrix {
				select {
				case ForceLedgerCommit <- struct{}{}:
				default:
				}
			}
		}

		p.mu.Lock()
		slot.State = SlotStateIdle
		slot.ActiveJob = nil
		p.mu.Unlock()
	}
}


// GetStatus returns the current state of the planner, queue and slots
func (p *Planner) GetStatus() map[string]interface{} {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()

	slotStatuses := []map[string]interface{}{}
	for _, s := range p.slots {
		var activeJob interface{}
		if s.ActiveJob != nil {
			activeJob = map[string]interface{}{
				"id":       s.ActiveJob.ID,
				"priority": s.ActiveJob.Priority,
				"type":     s.ActiveJob.Type.String(),
				"compiler": s.ActiveJob.CompilerTag,
				"jobs":     len(s.ActiveJob.Jobs),
				"current":  s.CurrentTask,
			}
		}
		slotStatuses = append(slotStatuses, map[string]interface{}{
			"id":         s.ID,
			"state":      s.State,
			"active_job": activeJob,
		})
	}

	queueSnapshot := []map[string]interface{}{}
	for _, wp := range p.Packages {
		queueSnapshot = append(queueSnapshot, map[string]interface{}{
			"id":       wp.ID,
			"priority": wp.Priority,
			"type":     wp.Type.String(),
			"compiler": wp.CompilerTag,
			"jobs":     len(wp.Jobs),
		})
	}

	inboxSnapshot := []map[string]interface{}{}
	for _, wp := range p.inbox {
		inboxSnapshot = append(inboxSnapshot, map[string]interface{}{
			"id":       wp.ID,
			"priority": wp.Priority,
			"type":     wp.Type.String(),
			"compiler": wp.CompilerTag,
			"jobs":     len(wp.Jobs),
		})
	}

	return map[string]interface{}{
		"planner_state": p.State,
		"inbox_size":    len(p.inbox),
		"inbox":         inboxSnapshot,
		"slots":         slotStatuses,
		"queue":         queueSnapshot,
		"next_commit":   p.NextLedgerCommit,
	}
}

// rejectPR reports a CI infrastructure failure for the given PR and returns nil
// to signal that the WorkPackage should be dropped. This is a fail-fast strategy:
// a broken registry is an infrastructure error that must surface immediately,
// not be silently masked by a "latest" fallback that produces misleading results.
func (p *Planner) rejectPR(wp *WorkPackage, reason string) []*WorkPackage {
	prData := wp.Jobs[0].Payload.(PRCheckPayload)
	log.Printf("[Planner] ERROR: Rejecting PR #%d — %s", prData.PRNumber, reason)
	reportStatusToGitHub(prData.RepoName, prData.Sha, "error",
		fmt.Sprintf("CI infrastructure error: %s", reason))
	return nil
}

func (p *Planner) buildPRTestPlan(wp *WorkPackage) []*WorkPackage {
	prData := wp.Jobs[0].Payload.(PRCheckPayload)
	log.Printf("[Planner] Expanding PR Test Plan for PR #%d", prData.PRNumber)

	registryDir := "/repo-registry"
	regData, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(registryDir, "registry.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return p.rejectPR(wp, fmt.Sprintf("cannot read registry.json: %v", err))
	}

	var registry struct {
		Ecosystem struct {
			Cli      []string `json:"cli"`
			CoreSDK  []string `json:"core_sdk"`
			Compiler []string `json:"compiler"`
		} `json:"ecosystem"`
		Chips map[string]struct {
			Version string `json:"version"`
		} `json:"chips"`
	}

	if err := json.Unmarshal(regData, &registry); err != nil {
		return p.rejectPR(wp, fmt.Sprintf("malformed registry.json: %v", err))
	}

	if len(registry.Ecosystem.Compiler) == 0 || len(registry.Ecosystem.CoreSDK) == 0 {
		return p.rejectPR(wp, "registry.json missing ecosystem data (compiler/core_sdk lists empty)")
	}

	latestCompiler := registry.Ecosystem.Compiler[len(registry.Ecosystem.Compiler)-1]
	latestCore := registry.Ecosystem.CoreSDK[len(registry.Ecosystem.CoreSDK)-1]

	var packages []*WorkPackage

	// 1. "Latest Version" - completely test all chips with the latest compiler
	latestWP := &WorkPackage{
		ID:          fmt.Sprintf("%s-latest", wp.ID),
		Priority:    1,
		Type:        JobTypePRCheck,
		CompilerTag: latestCompiler,
		CreatedAt:   wp.CreatedAt,
		Jobs:        []Job{},
	}
	
	for chipKey, chipMeta := range registry.Chips {
		latestWP.Jobs = append(latestWP.Jobs, Job{
			ID:    GenerateComboID("PR", chipKey, chipMeta.Version, "main", latestCore, latestCompiler),
			Type:  JobTypePRCheck,
			RunID: wp.ID,
			Payload: PRCheckPayload{
				PRNumber:        prData.PRNumber,
				RepoName:        prData.RepoName,
				Sha:             prData.Sha,
				Chips:           []string{chipKey},
				CoreVersion:     latestCore,
				CompilerVersion: latestCompiler,
			},
		})
	}
	packages = append(packages, latestWP)

	// 2. "Historical Sample" - randomized from SQLite ledger
	if GlobalStore != nil {
		sampled, err := GlobalStore.QueryHistoricalSample(latestCompiler, 5)
		if err == nil {
			// Group sampled targets by CompilerTag into WorkPackages
			groups := make(map[string]*WorkPackage)
			for _, target := range sampled {
				gwp, exists := groups[target.Compiler]
				if !exists {
					gwp = &WorkPackage{
						ID:          fmt.Sprintf("%s-hist-%s", wp.ID, target.Compiler),
						Priority:    1,
						Type:        JobTypePRCheck,
						CompilerTag: target.Compiler,
						CreatedAt:   wp.CreatedAt,
						Jobs:        []Job{},
					}
					groups[target.Compiler] = gwp
				}
				
				gwp.Jobs = append(gwp.Jobs, Job{
					ID:    GenerateComboID("PR", target.Chip, target.ChipVersion, "main", target.Core, target.Compiler),
					Type:  JobTypePRCheck,
					RunID: wp.ID,
					Payload: PRCheckPayload{
						PRNumber:        prData.PRNumber,
						RepoName:        prData.RepoName,
						Sha:             prData.Sha,
						Chips:           []string{target.Chip},
						CoreVersion:     target.Core,
						CompilerVersion: target.Compiler,
					},
				})
			}
			
			for _, gwp := range groups {
				packages = append(packages, gwp)
			}
		} else {
			log.Printf("[Planner] Failed to query historical sample: %v", err)
		}
	}
	
	// Track the total number of jobs for the PR Run
	totalJobs := 0
	for _, p := range packages {
		totalJobs += len(p.Jobs)
	}
	
	p.mu.Lock()
	p.runs[wp.ID] = &Run{
		ID:        wp.ID,
		Source:    "pr",
		TotalJobs: totalJobs,
		StartedAt: time.Now().Unix(),
	}
	p.mu.Unlock()

	return packages
}


======================================================================
FILE: cli/.pipeline-repo/toob-ci/ports.go
======================================================================

// ports.go — Interface Contract Registry for the Toob-CI Orchestrator.
//
// This file is the Single Source of Truth for every interface that
// crosses a boundary of the Orchestrator component.
//
// NOTE: These structs serve primarily as documentation contracts.
// While they define the exact expected shapes of API payloads and JSON formats,
// they are not currently enforced at runtime in all handlers. This allows for
// flexibility and avoids massive refactoring, while still maintaining a clear
// schema definition for developers.
//
// Contract Rules:
//
//	Adding a `port:"required"` field   = BREAKING
//	Removing any field                 = BREAKING
//	Changing a field type              = BREAKING
//	Adding a `port:"optional"` field   = non-breaking
package main

// =============================================================================
// Boundary: Orchestrator → Compiler Container (via Docker)
// =============================================================================

// SessionStartInput defines the parameters for spawning a Compiler Container.
type SessionStartInput struct {
	ContainerName string `json:"container_name" port:"required"`
	CompilerTag   string `json:"compiler_tag"   port:"required"` // toob-compiler:{tag}
	SecurityMode  string `json:"security_mode"  port:"required"` // "PR" | "MATRIX"
	ReadOnly      bool   `json:"read_only"      port:"required"` // --read-only
	NoNewPriv     bool   `json:"no_new_priv"    port:"required"` // --security-opt no-new-privileges:true
	MemoryLimit   string `json:"memory_limit"   port:"required"` // --memory=1536m
	TmpfsTmp      string `json:"tmpfs_tmp"      port:"required"` // --tmpfs /tmp:rw,exec,size=512m
	TmpfsToob     string `json:"tmpfs_toob"     port:"required"` // --tmpfs /root/.toob:rw,size=256m
	ToolchainVol  string `json:"toolchain_vol"  port:"required"` // toob_toolchain_cache:/root/.toob/toolchains
	WorkspaceVol  string `json:"workspace_vol"  port:"optional"` // -v /repo:/workspace:ro (PR mode only)
	Entrypoint    string `json:"entrypoint"     port:"required"` // sleep infinity
}

// SessionExecInput defines the parameters for a single chip build.
type SessionExecInput struct {
	ContainerName   string `json:"container_name"    port:"required"`
	Chip            string `json:"chip"              port:"required"`
	BuildMode       string `json:"build_mode"        port:"required"` // TOOB_BUILD_MODE=pr|matrix
	CoreVersion     string `json:"core_version"      port:"required"` // TOOB_CORE_VERSION
	CompilerVersion string `json:"compiler_version"  port:"required"` // TOOB_COMPILER_VERSION
	Command         string `json:"command"           port:"required"` // toob-ci-build {chip}
}

// SessionExecOutput defines what the container returns after a build.
type SessionExecOutput struct {
	ExitCode int    `json:"exit_code" port:"required"` // 0 = VERIFIED, non-zero = FAILED
	Output   []byte `json:"output"    port:"required"` // Combined stdout+stderr
}

// SessionLogsOutput defines the docker logs archival.
type SessionLogsOutput struct {
	LogPath string `json:"log_path" port:"optional"` // /cache/logs/session_{name}.log
	LogData []byte `json:"log_data" port:"optional"`
}

// =============================================================================
// Boundary: Orchestrator → GitHub API
// =============================================================================

// GitHubStatusReport defines the POST body for commit status updates.
type GitHubStatusReport struct {
	Repo        string `json:"repo"        port:"required"` // {owner}/{repo}
	SHA         string `json:"sha"         port:"required"` // Commit SHA
	State       string `json:"state"       port:"required"` // "success" | "failure"
	Description string `json:"description" port:"required"`
	Context     string `json:"context"     port:"required"` // "Toob-CI Daemon"
	Token       string `json:"-"           port:"required"` // RELEASE_TARGET_TOKEN (never serialized)
}

// VersionIndexFetch defines the polling of version_index.json from GitHub.
type VersionIndexFetch struct {
	URL      string `json:"url"      port:"required"` // raw.githubusercontent.com/.../version_index.json
	Interval string `json:"interval" port:"required"` // 10 minutes
}

// MatrixFetch defines the compatibility_matrix.json schema.
type MatrixDependencies struct {
	Toolchain string `json:"toolchain"          port:"required"`
	Vendor    string `json:"vendor"             port:"required"`
	Arch      string `json:"arch"               port:"required"`
	Compiler  string `json:"compiler_container" port:"optional"`
	CoreSDK   string `json:"core_sdk"           port:"optional"`
}

type MatrixVerifiedCli struct {
	Status     string `json:"status"      port:"required"`
	LastTested string `json:"last_tested" port:"required"`
}

type MatrixVersion struct {
	EnvironmentHash     string                       `json:"environment_hash"      port:"required"`
	Dependencies        MatrixDependencies           `json:"dependencies"          port:"required"`
	VerifiedCliVersions map[string]MatrixVerifiedCli `json:"verified_cli_versions" port:"required"`
}

type MatrixChip struct {
	Versions map[string]MatrixVersion `json:"versions" port:"required"`
}

type Matrix map[string]MatrixChip

// =============================================================================
// Boundary: GitHub → Orchestrator (Webhooks)
// =============================================================================

// WebhookReleasePayload defines the expected fields from a release webhook.
type WebhookReleasePayload struct {
	Ref      string `json:"ref"       port:"required"` // refs/tags/cli/v0.0.15
	RefType  string `json:"ref_type"  port:"required"` // "tag"
	RepoName string `json:"repo_name" port:"required"` // Toob-Boot/Toob-Loader
	SHA      string `json:"sha"       port:"required"` // Commit SHA
}

// WebhookPRPayload defines the expected fields from a PR webhook.
type WebhookPRPayload struct {
	Action   string   `json:"action"    port:"required"` // "opened" | "synchronize"
	PRNumber int      `json:"pr_number" port:"required"`
	RepoName string   `json:"repo_name" port:"required"`
	SHA      string   `json:"sha"       port:"required"` // Head commit SHA
	Chips    []string `json:"chips"     port:"optional"` // Chips to test (auto-detected)
}

// =============================================================================
// Boundary: Orchestrator → Registry (file I/O + git)
// =============================================================================

// MatrixResultFile defines the per-build result file written to the outbox.
type MatrixResultFile struct {
	Chip            string `json:"chip"             port:"required"`
	ChipVersion     string `json:"chip_version"     port:"optional"` // chip_manifest.json version at build time
	CLIVersion      string `json:"cli_version"      port:"required"`
	CoreVersion     string `json:"core_version"     port:"required"`
	CompilerVersion string `json:"compiler_version" port:"required"`
	Status          string `json:"status"           port:"required"` // "VERIFIED" | "FAILED"
	StateHash       string `json:"state_hash"       port:"required"`
	ErrorType       string `json:"error_type"       port:"optional"` // "INFRA_ERROR" | "COMPILER_ERROR"
}

// InternalStateEntry defines a single entry in internal_state.json.
type InternalStateEntry struct {
	Chip            string `json:"chip"             port:"required"`
	ChipVersion     string `json:"chip_version"     port:"optional"` // chip_manifest.json version at build time
	CoreVersion     string `json:"core_version"     port:"required"`
	CompilerVersion string `json:"compiler_version" port:"required"`
	ErrorType       string `json:"error_type"       port:"required"`
	LastAttempt     int64  `json:"last_attempt"     port:"required"` // Unix timestamp
}

// RegistryPushOperation defines the git push to the Toob-Registry.
type RegistryPushOperation struct {
	RepoPath   string `json:"repo_path"   port:"required"` // /repo-registry
	Branch     string `json:"branch"      port:"required"` // main
	CommitMsg  string `json:"commit_msg"  port:"required"` // "[toob-ci] ..."
	AuthToken  string `json:"-"           port:"required"` // RELEASE_TARGET_TOKEN
}

// =============================================================================
// Boundary: Release Pipelines (GitHub Actions)
// =============================================================================

// CLIReleasePipeline defines the release-cli.yml contract.
type CLIReleasePipeline struct {
	Trigger    string   `json:"trigger"     port:"required"` // push tag cli/v*
	Targets    []string `json:"targets"     port:"required"` // windows/amd64, linux/amd64, darwin/amd64, darwin/arm64
	OutputRepo string   `json:"output_repo" port:"required"` // Toob-Boot/Toob-CLI-Release
	LDFlags    string   `json:"ldflags"     port:"required"` // -X cmd.Version={version}
}

type CoreReleasePipeline struct {
	Trigger       string   `json:"trigger"        port:"required"` // push tag core/v*

	ArchiveFormat string   `json:"archive_format" port:"required"` // toob-core-v{ver}.zip
}

// CompilerReleasePipeline defines the release-compiler.yml contract.
type CompilerReleasePipeline struct {
	Trigger    string `json:"trigger"    port:"required"` // push tag compiler/v*
	Dockerfile string `json:"dockerfile" port:"required"` // Dockerfile.compiler
